Result: 在KITTI测试序列上达到93-100%的吞吐量满足率，节省76%能耗；在未见过的nuScenes数据集上保持97%吞吐量满足率，能耗比SOTA降低64%。

Conclusion: TAPAS证明了通过运行时自适应资源分配可以有效平衡动态场景下的吞吐量需求和能耗，具备良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous systems rely on a perception module to navigate through dynamic environments. In real-world scenarios, the perception module's throughput requirements vary at runtime due to changes in scene complexity. However, existing perception strategies assume a fixed FPS and static model-to-cluster mapping, resulting in either over/under provision of throughput requirements or unnecessary energy consumption across diverse scenes. Addressing this challenge requires tightly coupled \textit{scene complexity awareness} to estimate an appropriate FPS target and \textit{dynamic model-to-cluster mapping} to deliver the required throughput at minimum energy. We propose a throughput-adaptive perception strategy for mobile/edge platforms, enabling intelligent runtime resource allocation based on varying FPS targets. We use Reinforcement Learning (RL) with RRM (Reward Reasoning Model) and a GRU (Gated Recurrent Unit) agent to orchestrate perception tasks across heterogeneous mobile/edge platforms. We evaluate TAPAS on Jetson Orin NX across KITTI and unseen nuScenes. On the \textit{KITTI} dataset's test sequences, TAPAS achieves 93-100% throughput met rate while saving energy by 76%. On the unseen \textit{nuScenes} dataset, TAPAS maintains 97% throughput met rate with 64% lower energy compared to \textit{SOTA} approaches, proving its robustness.

</details>


### [108] [Rethinking the Suitability of Reinforcement Learning Algorithms Under Practical Transfer Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17326)
*Hany Hamed,Abhishek Naik,Colin Bellinger,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了迁移强化学习中，除样本效率外，还应考虑实际训练时间和领域随机化对策略鲁棒性的影响。实验表明，样本效率较低的PPO在并行训练下比SAC和TD-MPC2更快获得可用策略，且领域随机化对三种不同范式算法的影响相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移强化学习需要超越标准样本效率的评估维度，现有研究缺乏对实际训练时间和领域随机化鲁棒性的系统比较。

Method: 在相同迁移协议下，对PPO（在线）、SAC（离线）和TD-MPC2（基于模型）三种不同范式的算法进行控制变量比较实验，评估实际训练时间效率和领域随机化覆盖范围的影响。

Result: 1) 样本效率低的PPO在并行训练下实际更快获得可用策略；2) 领域随机化对三种不同范式算法的影响模式相似。

Conclusion: 迁移强化学习中应综合评估样本效率、实际训练时间和策略可用性等多维指标，而非仅依赖样本效率。

Abstract: Transfer-oriented reinforcement learning requires evaluating algorithms along dimensions that go beyond standard sample efficiency. We focus on two dimensions: practical efficiency, which asks whether conclusions about algorithm suitability change under wall-clock rather than interaction-based budgets, and robustness under dynamics mismatch, which asks how different learning paradigms respond to variability in the training distribution induced by domain randomization. We provide two insights to reinforcement-learning practitioners. First, comparing the sample efficiency of different algorithms is often an insufficient criterion in transfer-oriented settings. The wall-clock time required to train a decent policy is an important consideration for practitioners, and we find that the sample-inefficient PPO algorithm can produce a performant policy faster than relatively more sample-efficient algorithms such as SAC and TD-MPC2, validating the common understanding of massively parallel training paradigms. Second, domain randomization can help different kinds of algorithms learn robust policies. In particular, although PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2 represent different RL paradigms - on-policy, off-policy, and model-based learning and planning, respectively - we find that domain randomization affects all three algorithms in a similar way. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first controlled comparison of the effect of domain-randomization coverage on PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2 under the same transfer protocol. Taken together, these two insights highlight the importance of evaluating RL algorithms not only by sample efficiency, but also by practical considerations such as training time and the algorithms' ability to produce usable policies.

</details>


### [109] [When Drift Detectors cry Wolf: False Alarm Rates in continuous ML Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17336)
*Raj Shekhar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种常用漂移检测器（PSI、KS、MMD、LSDD和对抗验证）在连续监控条件下的假阳性行为，发现PSI在小批量时假阳性率高但随样本量增大（>200）显著改善，而KS、MMD和LSDD在不同批量下持续波动但小样本时更可靠，Bonferroni校正虽降低假阳性率但削弱了检测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漂移检测研究多关注合成偏移下的检测准确性，忽视了生产环境中连续监控导致的假阳性累积问题（报警疲劳），缺乏对假阳性行为的系统实证分析。

Method: 对五种常用漂移检测器（PSI、KS、MMD、LSDD、对抗验证）进行经验性分析，比较不同批量大小下的假阳性率，并评估Bonferroni校正对假阳性-灵敏度权衡的影响。

Result: PSI在小样本时假阳性频繁，批量>200后稳定性显著改善；KS、MMD、LSDD在各批量下持续波动但小样本时优于PSI；Bonferroni校正降低假阳性率但损失真阳性灵敏度。

Conclusion: 验证了漂移检测中稳定性与灵敏度之间的固有权衡，为生产ML系统中漂移检测器的选择和校准提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Drift detection is a core component of production machine learning monitoring systems, where detectors are used to compare incoming data with a reference distribution and trigger alerts when changes occur. However, these detectors are often evaluated in research settings that emphasize detection accuracy under synthetic shifts, while overlooking false alarms under continuous monitoring. In production environments, models are monitored repeatedly over time and across many features, and even small false positive rates can accumulate into frequent alerts, leading to alarm fatigue. We empirically analyze false positive behavior across five commonly used drift detectors: PSI, KS, MMD, LSDD, and adversarial validation. Consistent with existing literature, PSI exhibits strong sensitivity to batch size, producing frequent false alarms at small sample sizes; however, we further observe that its behavior stabilizes and improves substantially once batch sizes exceed approximately 200 samples. In contrast, KS, MMD, and LSDD display persistent fluctuations across batch sizes, while remaining comparatively more reliable than PSI in low-data regimes. Applying a Bonferroni correction reduces false positive rates, but often at the cost of reduced true positive sensitivity, reinforcing the well-known stability - sensitivity trade-off in drift detection. This work provides a systematic comparison of false positive behavior across multiple drift detectors under continuous monitoring conditions. We identify tradeoffs across detector families and provide practical guidelines for selecting and calibrating drift detectors in production ML systems.

</details>


### [110] [A multiverse-consensus pipeline for reproducible feature selection in untargeted LC-MS metabolomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17345)
*Mohammed Saeed Al-Huraibi,Ihsan Yozgat,Ahmet Kaplan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对非靶向LC-MS代谢组学中预处理决策不确定性导致特征选择不可靠的问题，提出了多宇宙分析框架，通过可审计的配置驱动流程，在不同预处理路径和排序方法下构建共识特征集，并在乳腺癌细胞系数据集上验证了该方法能有效降低假阳性并提升结果稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非靶向代谢组学分析中，预处理决策（如归一化、漂移校正等）存在大量自由度，不同分析管道可能产生差异极大的特征短名单（Jaccard系数低至0.05），而传统研究中分析师通常只报告单一管道结果，隐藏了这些决策对结论的影响。本文旨在通过多宇宙分析显式量化并缓解这一不确定性。

Method: 提出了一个包含两阶段的多宇宙分析框架：(i) 十阶段质量控制过滤级联，记录每个特征的保留/丢弃轨迹；(ii) 下游分析构建多宇宙，组合4种预处理哲学（归一化/漂移校正方法不同）与4种特征排序方法（含Bootstrap稳定性选择和标签置换检验），仅保留跨路径重复出现的特征进入分层共识集。

Result: 在5种乳腺癌细胞系数据集（30,370个检测特征）上，单一管道返回4-20个特征，两两一致Jaccard系数最低仅0.05；多宇宙共识保留15个特征（≥2/4路径），其中1个在所有4条路径中重现；全管道标签置换检验在50次零置换中未发现任何假阳性。

Conclusion: 报告仅经预处理鲁棒的特征并附带完整的保留/丢弃审计轨迹，将隐藏的分析自由度转化为显式可检查的输出。讨论了该方法在单批次设计和需要独立验证方面的局限性。

Abstract: Background: Untargeted LC-MS metabolomics requires a long chain of preprocessing decisions, each with several equally defensible options. Analysts typically commit to one pipeline and report the resulting feature shortlist. How strongly that shortlist depends on choices that were never varied stays invisible. Results: We adapt multiverse analysis to untargeted metabolomics feature selection. We present an auditable, configuration-driven pipeline that (i) applies a ten-stage quality-control filter cascade in which every feature's fate is logged, and (ii) runs the downstream analysis as a multiverse over four contrasting preprocessing philosophies, each combined with four feature-ranking methods under bootstrap stability selection and label-permutation testing. Only features recurring across paths enter a tiered consensus. On a demonstration dataset of five breast-cancer cell lines (30,370 detected features), the four single pipelines individually returned shortlists of 4-20 features whose pairwise agreement was as low as Jaccard = 0.05. The multiverse consensus retained 15 features (>=2/4 paths), of which one recurred across all four, although two paths (sharing normalization and drift-correction methods) dominate the consensus. A pipeline-wide label-permutation test found no false discoveries in 50 null permutations. Conclusions: Reporting only preprocessing-robust features, with a complete kept/dropped audit trail, converts hidden analytical degrees of freedom into an explicit, inspectable output. We discuss scope and limitations, including single-batch design and the need for independent validation.

</details>


### [111] [Chebyshev Manifold Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17377)
*Jiawen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChebyMA（切比雪夫流形适配）方法，通过可学习坐标上的切比雪夫多项式基的多曲面叠加来近似权重矩阵，替代标准线性投影，在CIFAR和NLP数据集上取得优于全参数微调、LoRA等方法的参数-精度Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效适配方法（如LoRA）表达能力有限，难以在高维复杂特征解耦中取得良好效果。本文从函数逼近理论出发，希望设计一种兼具高表达能力和参数效率的适配方法。

Method: ChebyMA采用可学习坐标上的切比雪夫多项式基，通过多曲面（S>1）叠加并由可训练系数矩阵组合，用连续函数逼近替代标准线性投影。理论层面证明了单流形ChebyMA的Frobenius范数重构误差收敛性，并基于Kolmogorov n-宽度论证了多流形叠加的表达式优势。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、AG News、SST-2数据集上，ChebyMA在参数-精度Pareto前沿上一致优于全参数微调、LoRA、TLoRA和StelLA，且显著超越其他对比方法。

Conclusion: ChebyMA具有坚实的函数逼近理论基础，通过纯向量化计算实现高效通用适配，验证了其在多种任务中的泛化能力。

Abstract: The paper presents a new parameter-efficient adaptation method called ChebyMA (Chebyshev Manifold Adaptation). ChebyMA adopts weight matrices through a multi-surface superposition of Chebyshev polynomial bases evaluated on learnable coordinates and combined via trainable coefficient matrices, replacing standard linear projections with highly expressive continuous function approximation. Theoretically, we establish an Approximation Expressivity Theorem, proving from the perspective of function approximation theory that single-manifold ChebyMA guarantees convergence in Frobenius norm error of reconstruction. Besides, drawing on Kolmogorov $n$-width intuition, we demonstrate the expressive advantages of multi-manifold superposition ($S > 1$) in decoupling high-dimensional complex features. Experimental results on Computer Vision CIFAR datasets(CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100)\cite{CIFAR} and Natural Language Processing (AG News, SST-2) datasets demonstrate that ChebyMA consistently achieves a superior parameter-accuracy Pareto front compared to standard full-parameter fine-tuning, LoRA\cite{LoRA}, TLoRA\cite{TLoRA}, and StelLA\cite{StelLA}. ChebyMA significantly outperforms other tested methods in tested datasets, validating its solid theoretical foundation for generality with purely vectorized computations.

</details>


### [112] [CoEvoP&R: Co-Evolving Placement Objectives with Routing Feedback via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17398)
*Ruogu Chen,Weihua Xiao,Ramesh Karri,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoEvoP&R 是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，能够自动演化分析性布局目标函数，通过将LLM提议的可微目标嵌入DREAMPlace并基于布线反馈进行迭代优化，在多个基准上将布线后线长和拥塞分别降低最高16.9%和36.7%，并显著改善时序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析性布局器依赖HPWL和单元密度惩罚等替代目标函数，这些中间代理指标与下游实际布线质量和时序质量存在对齐偏差。先前的解决方式要么依赖人工设计的项（需要专家重调），要么使用黑盒替代（难以解释、调试和部署在分析性布局流程中），缺乏自动、可解释且有效的优化方法。

Method: 提出CoEvoP&R框架：每一代中，将受限目标接口、基线上下文和存档的先前候选方案与来自布局、时序代理和布线工具的布线相关反馈组合成提示，输入LLM；LLM提议可读的可微目标函数，将其嵌入DREAMPlace中进行验证，并通过时序代理和实际路由器评估效果，反馈存储用于指导后续代际进化。

Result: 在8个ChiP-Bench Nangate45设计上，相比原生DREAMPlace，布线后线长降低16.9%，拥塞降低36.7%，最差负松弛改善0.70ns，总负松弛量减少912ns。在8个ICCAD 2015 Superblue设计上，布线后线长降低5.4%，拥塞降低23.2%。

Conclusion: CoEvoP&R通过LLM驱动的自动进化框架，有效弥合了分析性布局中替代目标与下游布线/时序质量之间的差距，实现了布线拥塞和时序指标的一致改进，且目标函数具有可读性和可解释性。

Abstract: Analytical placers rely on differentiable objective functions to guide placement, typically combining intermediate surrogate metrics such as half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) and cell-density penalties. However, these placement-stage surrogates remain misaligned with downstream routed and timing quality. Prior work reduces this gap with human-designed terms or learned black-box surrogates, but the former requires expert retuning and the latter is difficult to explain, debug, or deploy in analytical placement flows. CoEvoP&R addresses these limitations with a large language model (LLM)-based framework that automatically evolves analytical placement objectives. At each generation, the prompt combines the restricted objective interface, baseline context, and archived prior candidates with routing-related feedback from placement, timing proxy, and routing tools. The LLM proposes readable differentiable objectives, which are embedded and validated in DREAMPlace, evaluated through a timing proxy and an actual router, and stored with their feedback to guide later generations. Across eight ChiP-Bench Nangate45 designs and three seeds, CoEvoP&R reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 16.9% and 36.7%, with gains of 0.70 ns in worst negative slack and a 912 ns reduction in total negative slack magnitude over native DREAMPlace. Across eight ICCAD 2015 Superblue designs, it reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 5.4% and 23.2%. Code is available at https://github.com/FCHXWH823/CoEvoP-R.git.

</details>


### [113] [CORAL: Learning Amyloid Fibril Ligand Docking with Cooperative Binding Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17412)
*Yasheng Sun,Bohan Li,Youqi Tao,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CORAL，一种基于强化学习的生成式对接框架，专门用于解决淀粉样纤维配体结合模式预测问题。CORAL通过引入配体-配体协同堆叠能量和蛋白质-配体对接亲和度的联合奖励，捕捉交叉β沟槽中的独特结合几何特征，在实验结构和评估集上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淀粉样纤维（如阿尔茨海默病、帕金森病相关蛋白聚集体）的配体对接面临两大根本挑战：一是实验解析的共晶结构极其稀少，传统监督学习的对接模型难以训练；二是淀粉样纤维的配体结合模式与球状蛋白完全不同——配体嵌入纵向交叉β沟槽并沿纤维轴协同堆叠，现有对接模型无法捕捉这种几何特征。

Method: 提出CORAL（COopeRative Amyloid Ligand docking）框架，使用强化学习训练生成式对接模型。奖励函数创新性地结合了配体-配体协同堆叠能量和蛋白质-配体对接亲和度，直接建模淀粉样纤维的独特结合几何。此外，构建了由领域专家验证的模型生成构象组成的评估集。

Result: 在实验解析结构和所构建的专家验证评估集上，CORAL在配体构象质量和结合亲和度相关性方面均优于现有对接基线方法。

Conclusion: CORAL有效解决了淀粉样纤维这一特殊靶标类别的配体对接难题，为神经退行性疾病相关诊断探针、成像剂和治疗分子的设计提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: A hallmark of neurodegenerative diseases such as Alzheimer's and Parkinson's is the aberrant aggregation of proteins into amyloid fibrils, and small molecules that selectively bind to these fibrils hold promise as diagnostics, imaging probes, and therapeutics. Predicting how such ligands bind to fibril targets, however, presents two fundamental challenges. First, resolved co-crystal structures of amyloid-ligand complexes are exceptionally scarce; even with recent advances in cryo-EM only a handful have been structurally characterized, making supervised training of docking models impractical for this target class. Second, amyloid fibrils present a binding mode fundamentally different from globular proteins: ligands intercalate into longitudinal cross-$β$ grooves and stack cooperatively along the fibril axis, a geometry that existing docking models are not designed to capture. To address these challenges, we present CORAL (COopeRative Amyloid Ligand docking), a reinforcement learning framework that trains a generative docking model to produce ligand pose distributions tailored to the cross-$β$ groove geometry. Our reward explicitly incorporates cooperative ligand-ligand stacking energy alongside protein-ligand docking affinity, directly capturing the distinctive binding geometry of amyloid fibrils. We further introduce a curated evaluation set of amyloid-ligand complexes constructed from model-generated poses validated by domain experts. Experiments on both experimentally resolved structures and this evaluation set demonstrate improved pose quality and binding affinity correlation over existing docking baselines.

</details>


### [114] [Grounded verification of chemical and materials reasoning: detection is the bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17417)
*Can Polat,Mustafa Kurban,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种分层验证器框架，通过从大语言模型推理轨迹中提取可验证的化学声明，与权威数据库和物理规则进行核对，并采用门控修正循环修复错误。在528个条件锚定提示和4个模型上，该方法将公式错误率从22%降至4%，检索次数比全面增强减少3.2倍，修复成功率达到80-97%，瓶颈在于循环内的检测召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在化学推理中会虚构化学对象（分子式、空间群、形成能等），尤其在长尾实体上置信度低但错误集中。现有方法存在覆盖成本高或检测效率低的问题，需要一种高效、可验证的错误检测与修复机制。

Method: 提出分层验证器（tiered verifier），流程包括：1) 从推理轨迹中提取每个可检查的声明；2) 对照权威数据库和物理规则进行验证；3) 将验证结果引用纳入门控修正循环（gated correction loop），仅在检测到错误时才触发修复。

Result: 在4个模型、528个条件锚定提示上，门控修正将承诺公式错误率从22%降至4%；检索次数比全面增强（blanket augmentation）减少3.2倍，甚至优于对话式Oracle。修复成功率达80-97%，瓶颈在于循环内检测召回率。评估基准提高仅在验证器范围覆盖到交付物时出现（83%→90%），提升仅存在于可提取的长尾错误区域：物理常数接近上限时无提升，同位素半衰期从11%降至0%。

Conclusion: 确定性、数据库驱动的验证可以有效捕获和修复大语言模型在化学领域的虚构错误，瓶颈在于检测而非修复。门控修正策略在保持低检索成本的同时显著降低错误率，验证器的范围设计对最终性能提升至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models confabulate chemical objects (molecular formulas, space groups, formation energies) in fluent reasoning traces, concentrated on long-tail entities where confidence is least trustworthy. Deterministic, database-grounded verification can catch and repair such errors without the coverage cost of blanket retrieval; the binding constraint, we find, is detection, not repair. Our tiered verifier extracts each checkable claim, checks it against authoritative databases and physics, and feeds the reference into a gated correction loop. Across four models and 528 condition-pinned prompts, gated correction cuts committed-formula error from 22% to 4% at $3.2\times$ fewer retrievals than blanket augmentation, beating a conversational oracle. Repair succeeds wherever a flag fires (80--97%); the bottleneck is in-loop detection recall. Grounding improves the final answer only when the verifier's scope reaches the deliverable (83% to 90%), and the lift appears only where extractable long-tail error exists: absent on near-ceiling physical constants, large on isotope half-lives (11% to 0%).

</details>


### [115] [Kernelized Linear Attention: Breaking the Capacity Wall with Symmetric Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17419)
*Ayoub Ghriss,Sourav Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KATA框架，通过将注意力召回问题形式化为球面填充问题，利用自对偶齐次锥推导出具有非负注意力权重的核化线性注意力激活函数。秩一正半定特征在容量与干扰之间取得有利权衡，实现无参数凸输出门，并通过Welch干扰界刻画关联容量。KATA在131k tokens上达到约11倍FlashAttention-2前向吞吐量，在MQAR任务上优于Gated DeltaNet，并在340M参数LLM上验证了特征相关的流畅性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力在常数时间循环推理方面具有优势，但在关联召回任务上性能严重退化。现有方法缺乏从第一性原理出发设计特征映射的理论框架，导致容量与干扰之间的权衡不清晰。

Method: 将注意力召回形式化为球面填充问题，通过自对偶齐次锥认证非负注意力权重，提出KATA框架。采用秩一正半定特征，引入Welch干扰界刻画关联容量，设计融合Triton核实现FlashAttention风格前向和精确O(T)分块状态两种操作模式。

Result: KATA在131k tokens上达到约11倍FlashAttention-2前向吞吐量；平均约2.4倍匹配的序贯线性注意力基线吞吐量；在长程MQAR和重复键覆盖任务上多项变体优于Gated DeltaNet；在16倍分布外长度上保持0.985 MQAR，接近使用约1/4 KV缓存条目的softmax注意力。

Conclusion: KATA从第一性原理出发建立了线性注意力特征映射的理论框架，在保持高效推理的同时显著提升了关联召回能力。340M参数LLM实验揭示了特征相关的流畅性权衡，以及位置编码、delta规则和衰减门与特征几何的交互作用。

Abstract: Linear attention promises constant-time recurrent inference but degrades sharply on associative recall. We formulate attention recall as a spherical-packing problem and introduce Kernelized Linear Attention Activations (KATA), a framework whose feature maps are derived from first principles by certifying nonnegative attention weights through a self-dual homogeneous cone. Building on this observation, we show that rank-one positive semi-definite (PSD) features offer a favorable capacity--interference tradeoff. KATA recovers a parameter-free convex output gate and characterizes associative capacity through the Welch interference floor. For tolerances above this floor, KATA enlarges the state without adding parameters and admits spherical codes with exponentially many keys in the projection dimension. We implement KATA as fused Triton kernels at two operating points: a flash-attention-style forward up to ${\sim}1.6\times$ FlashAttention-2 throughput, and an exact $O(T)$ chunked-state form that reaches ${\sim}11\times$ FlashAttention-2 forward throughput at $131$k tokens. An associative scan of the first-order feature lowers the inter-chunk recurrence depth to $O(\log(T/C))$ for chunk size $C$ and averages ${\sim}2.4\times$ the throughput of a matched sequential linear-attention baseline. On long-range MQAR and repeated-key overwrite, several KATA variants outperform Gated DeltaNet, with parameter counts and state sizes reported alongside accuracy. Induction preserves near-perfect recall, while kernel benchmarks show that the maps can be implemented efficiently. KATA retains $0.985$ MQAR at a $16\times$ out-of-distribution length, approaching the softmax with roughly one quarter of the KV-cache entries. Experiments on 340M-parameter LLMs reveal a feature-dependent fluency trade-off and clarify how positional embeddings, delta rules, and decay gates interact with feature geometry.

</details>


### [116] [Decoder-Preserving Sparse Autoencoders: Which Readouts Survive Sparse Compression?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17425)
*Aniket Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解码器保持的稀疏自编码器（DPSAE），在保持重建误差不变的前提下，通过引入最优岭回归预测器之间的矩阵失真项，明确保护特定线性读出任务免受稀疏压缩的影响。实验表明该方法在GPT-2上降低了10.6-11.4%的保留解码器失真，但保护效果局限于任务子空间内的线性读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器（SAE）在同等重建误差和稀疏度下，可能保留不同线性可解码信号，存在歧义性。这种歧义意味着重建质量不能保证特定线性读出任务的保留，因此需要显式约束来保护下游任务。

Method: 将最优岭回归预测器之间的矩阵值失真形式化，并将其与重建损失结合训练解码器保持的SAE（DPSAE）。通过秩松弛分析：各向同性任务先验饱和各模式遗漏成本但不改变PCA排序，而结构化先验可改变保留模式。实验在GPT-2 small block 8和Pythia上进行验证。

Result: 在GPT-2 small block 8上，DPSAE在三个配对运行中保持重建NMSE不变，将保留解码器失真降低10.6-11.4%。通过平均自然文本输出KL非劣效检验，但Pythia上一个匹配对显示在限制为少量稀疏特征时无改进。

Conclusion: 重建质量不能决定哪些重新拟合的线性读出能在稀疏压缩中存活；读出保护与学习更清洁的基准概念或保留每个冻结模型行为是不同的目标。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) compress model activations into sparse codes, but equal reconstruction error and sparsity can preserve different linearly decodable signals. We formalize this ambiguity as a matrix-valued distortion between optimal ridge-prediction operators and train decoder-preserving SAEs by combining this distortion with reconstruction loss. In a rank relaxation, an isotropic task prior saturates per-mode omission costs without changing PCA's ordering, whereas a structured prior can change which modes are retained. A controlled sparse experiment shows that a declared prior protects held-out combinations from its task subspace. On GPT-2 small block 8, DPSAE reduces held-out decoder distortion by 10.6--11.4% across three paired runs while matching reconstruction NMSE. The same checkpoints pass an average natural-text output-KL noninferiority test, but one matched Pythia pair shows no improvement in probes restricted to a few sparse features. These results show that reconstruction quality does not determine which refitted linear readouts survive sparse compression, and that readout preservation is distinct from learning cleaner benchmark concepts or preserving every frozen-model behavior.

</details>


### [117] [Abliteration Is Not a Scalpel: Off-Target Effects of Refusal Removal on Decision Disposition Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17427)
*Aleksander Fafuła*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对Gemma-4-26B和Qwen3-30B两种MoE模型的权重去拒绝（abliteration）手术，发现该操作并非"干净"的——它会系统性改变模型的乐观倾向、自我辩护长度、不确定性语言使用以及置信度表达，且效应方向存在家族依赖性。同时，工具链污染在社区修改模型中普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证流行的"去审查"技术（abliteration）是否真的仅移除模型的拒绝回答行为，而不带来其他副作用。现有"未经审查"模型被广泛部署，但其权重修改的副作用未得到严格量化评估。

Method: 使用21600个不确定性决策任务（60只华沙证券交易所股票的周度涨跌预测），在冻结流水线中仅替换决策层模型，对比两个MoE模型家族（Gemma-4-26B和Qwen3-30B）的base版本与abliterated版本，使用周聚类bootstrap置信区间进行统计检验。

Result: 三个效应跨家族复现：abliterated模型更乐观（+12.2pp Gemma，+7.4pp Qwen）、自我辩护更长、强制自我批评中使用更少不确定性词汇。一个效应符号相反：Gemma变体置信度降低而Qwen变体置信度提高。无经济技能优势（超额收益为beta而非alpha）。发现两个污染渠道：量化器不匹配和过期聊天模板。

Conclusion: 部署"未经审查"模型意味着部署一个可测量的不同决策者，而非"基础模型减去拒绝回答"。工具链污染是社区修改检查点研究中的常见问题。

Abstract: Abliteration - deleting a model's refusal direction from its weights - is the standard recipe behind popular "uncensored" open-weight models. We show the surgery is not clean. As a disposition probe we use 21,600 decisions under uncertainty - weekly up/down calls on 60 Warsaw Stock Exchange equities over 18 weeks, replayed through a frozen pipeline so the decision-layer model is the only variable. The task elicits no refusals at all, so any between-arm delta is pure side effect. Holding provenance constant (official BF16 checkpoints, a single abliteration author, an identical serving stack, one byte-identical frozen prompt), we compare base and abliterated arms of two Mixture-of-Experts families, Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507. Three effects replicate across both families (weeks-clustered bootstrap CIs excluding zero): abliterated models are systematically more optimistic (+12.2 pp Gemma, +7.4 pp Qwen; the confirmed preregistered endpoint), justify themselves at greater length, and use fewer explicit uncertainty words in forced self-critiques (both exploratory). A fourth effect reverses sign: the same operation makes Gemma-abliterated less confident and Qwen-abliterated more (family CIs non-overlapping) - one weight surgery, opposite shifts in expressed confidence. Capability covariates rule out instruction-following degradation as the driver, and no arm shows economic skill: the apparent edge of abliterated arms is regime beta, not alpha. Our provenance audit also caught two independent contamination channels - a mismatched-quantizer pilot pair and a stale community chat template that silently mangled the rendered prompt - suggesting toolchain artifacts are the rule in studies of community-modified checkpoints. Whoever deploys an "uncensored" model as an agent is deploying a measurably different decision-maker, not the base model minus refusals.

</details>


### [118] [Calibrated Alzheimer's Conversion Risk in Mild Cognitive Impairment: Persistent Homology of Clinical Trajectories with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17442)
*Navin Bondade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将持久同调应用于轻度认知障碍（MCI）向阿尔茨海默病（AD）转化的纵向临床轨迹点云预测，并首次为此任务提供分裂共形个体风险保证。通过纠正五个数据泄漏源、结合拓扑特征与工程特征构建堆叠集成模型，在ADNI数据集上实现了AUC=0.840，外部验证AUC=0.879，共形预测覆盖率达90.4%。H0持久熵被提出作为认知衰退的拓扑生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCI向AD转化的预测模型缺乏个体水平的不确定性估计，且很少包含透明的泄漏审计。为解决这两个关键缺陷，本文引入拓扑数据分析方法，并首次在该任务上应用共形预测框架。

Method: 分析了ADNI中741名MCI受试者（240名转化者，32.4%），统一4年随访上限。纠正了五个泄漏源。结合Vietoris-Rips持久同调与子水平集代理、轨迹斜率和工程特征（共76个），构建堆叠集成模型，通过5折交叉验证评估。

Result: Cox和随机生存森林模型加入TDA特征后，一致性指数分别提升+0.045和+0.014；主嵌套AUC为0.840（同折边界0.866），外部AUC为0.879；H0持久熵为SHAP最重要特征，与APOE4剂量显著相关；交叉共形覆盖率为90.4%±2.2%，外部覆盖率为96.9%；七个亚组的假阴性率最大公平差距为0.092。

Conclusion: 提出H0持久熵作为认知衰退的拓扑生物标志物，并证明了经过泄漏审计和共形校准的管道在该任务上达到了具有竞争力的准确性，同时提供了前所未有的个体水平不确定性量化。

Abstract: Background. Predicting conversion from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to Alzheimer's disease (AD) is central to trial enrichment and care planning, yet existing models provide no individual-level uncertainty estimates and rarely include transparent leakage audits. We introduce the first application of persistent homology to longitudinal clinical trajectory point clouds for this task, and the first split-conformal individual risk guarantee for any AD-conversion model. Methods. We analysed 741 MCI subjects (240 converters, 32.4%) from ADNI with a uniform 4-year follow-up cap. Five leakage sources were corrected; without them a naive pipeline achieved AUC=0.934, inflated by +0.075. Vietoris-Rips persistent homology and sublevel-set proxies were combined with trajectory slopes and engineered features (76 total) in a stacking ensemble evaluated by 5-fold cross-validation. Results. Cox and Random Survival Forest models with TDA features achieved concordance C=0.799 and C=0.826 versus C=0.753 and C=0.812 without (+0.045 and +0.014). The primary nested AUC is 0.840 (same-fold bound 0.866); external AUC was 0.879 on a zero-overlap ADNI-2/GO/3 cohort. H0 persistence entropy was the top SHAP feature and significantly associated with APOE4 dosage (Spearman r=-0.191, p<0.0001, Bonferroni-corrected). Cross-conformal coverage was 90.4%+-2.2% (target 90%); empirical external coverage 96.9%. Maximum fairness gap in false-negative rate across seven subgroups was 0.092. Conclusions. We propose H0 persistence entropy as a topological biomarker of cognitive decline and demonstrate that a leakage-audited, conformally calibrated pipeline reaches competitive accuracy with individual-level uncertainty quantification not previously available for this task.

</details>


### [119] [Residual-Guided Multi-Resolution Refinement of Foundation Models: A Case Study in Drought Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17507)
*Wentao Gao,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Thuc Duy Le,Jixue Liu,Yanchang Zhao,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGMR是一种推理时框架，通过残差引导的多分辨率精炼策略，在不更新骨干网络参数的情况下，提升预训练时间序列基础模型（如TimesFM、TimeGPT、TabPFN）在区域气候预测（尤其干旱预测）中的性能，在多个站点上显著降低了MSE误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家气候学家采用多尺度时间分析和基于系统误差诊断的迭代精炼策略，而现有时间序列基础模型通常通过单次推理处理时序模式，缺乏这种结构化精炼能力。因此，需要一种方法在不重新训练模型的前提下，将专业气象领域的多尺度精炼策略融入预训练基础模型。

Method: RGMR（Residual-Guided Multi-Resolution Refinement）是一个推理时框架，采用从粗到细的结构化精炼流程。它利用残差信号引导模型在不同时间分辨率上迭代修正预测结果，且无需更新预训练模型的骨干参数，适用于多种模型架构。

Result: 在三个南澳大利亚站点和三个其他区域上，RGMR在三种TSFM骨干（TimesFM、TimeGPT、TabPFN）上均一致降低了测试集MSE。应用于TimesFM时，对南澳大利亚三个站点的一个月提前期SPEI预测MSE降低最高达18.9%，平均降低约18.7%。

Conclusion: RGMR为在区域气候预测工作流中部署免训练的时序基础模型提供了一条实用路径，通过推理时的多分辨率精炼显著提升了预报精度。

Abstract: Regional climate prediction presents unique challenges for time series foundation models, which typically process temporal patterns through single-pass inference. Expert climatologists, in contrast, employ multi-scale temporal analysis and iterative refinement based on systematic error diagnosis. We present RGMR (Residual-Guided Multi-Resolution Refinement), an inference-time framework that adapts pre-trained foundation models to perform structured coarse-to-fine refinement for climate forecasting without updating backbone parameters. Applied to drought forecasting using the Standardized Precipitation Evapotranspiration Index (SPEI), RGMR is architecture-agnostic across the three TSFM backbones evaluated per site (TimesFM, TimeGPT, TabPFN) and consistently lowers test-set MSE on three South Australian sites and three additional regions outside South Australia. Applied to TimesFM, the wrapper reduces one-month-ahead SPEI MSE by up to 18.9\% across the three South Australian sites (mean reduction $\approx$18.7\%). Overall, RGMR provides a practical route for deploying frozen TSFMs in regional climate forecasting workflows.

</details>


### [120] [Retrieval-Augmented Interpretable Learning: Towards Task-Specific Zero-Shot Models in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17508)
*Sazan Mahbub,Caleb Ellington,Zhiyuan Li,Yixin Yang,Souvik Kundu,Ben Lengerich,Eric P. Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAIL是一个概率元学习框架，通过从自然语言任务描述和存储的预测器中检索相关任务并传递系数空间结构，实现零样本和少样本的临床程序预测，同时提供特征级解释和不确定性量化，在长尾分布任务上零样本准确率73.4%，极低样本（2-4例）下仍保持73.2%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域预测任务呈现高度长尾分布，新临床目标频繁出现，传统监督模型在零样本或极少样本场景下无法训练或表现接近随机水平，而现有方法缺乏可解释性、不确定感知能力以及兼容人工监督的特性。

Method: RAIL是一个概率元学习框架，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 从自然语言任务描述中检索相关源任务；(2) 通过系数空间传递结构信息；(3) 在原始诊断特征空间生成新预测器。其概率公式对检索、模型系数和预测结果均提供不确定性估计，支持可靠性感知部署。

Result: 在长尾临床程序预测任务中，RAIL在零样本设置下达到73.4%准确率（此时监督模型无法训练），在极低样本（2-4例）设置下保持73.2%准确率（监督模型接近随机水平），且提供检索、不确定性和系数级别的诊断信息。

Conclusion: RAIL为构建可扩展的临床预测系统提供了可行路径，能在保持可解释性和可靠性的前提下适应新任务，尤其适用于需要人工监督的高风险医疗场景。

Abstract: We introduce Retrieval-Augmented Interpretable Learning (RAIL), a probabilistic meta-learning framework for zero-shot generation of task-specific interpretable models that synthesizes coefficient-space structure from natural-language task descriptions and a memory of previously learned task-specific predictors. RAIL retrieves related source tasks, transfers structure through coefficient space, and generates a new predictor in the original diagnostic-feature space, enabling zero-shot and few-shot clinical procedure prediction with feature-level explanations. Its probabilistic formulation provides uncertainty over retrieval, model coefficients, and predictions, supporting reliability-aware deployment: uncertain predictions or unstable explanations can be flagged for additional clinical review rather than treated as automatic decisions. This makes RAIL particularly suited for healthcare settings, where prediction tasks are highly long-tailed, new clinical targets arise frequently, and models must remain inspectable, uncertainty-aware, and compatible with human oversight. Across long-tailed clinical procedure prediction tasks, RAIL maintains reliable performance across data-availability regimes: it achieves 73.4% accuracy in the held-out zero-shot settings, where no supervised task-specific model can be trained, and remains near 73.2% accuracy in the extreme few-shot regime with only 2-4 examples, where supervised task-specific models perform close to chance. RAIL further benefits from clinically informed task representations and yields retrieval, uncertainty, and coefficient-level diagnostics that make model behavior more transparent. These results suggest a path toward scalable clinical prediction systems that can adapt to new tasks while preserving interpretability and reliability.

</details>


### [121] [Lightweight Wrappers for Adapting Time Series Foundation Models to Regional Drought Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17511)
*Wentao Gao,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Thuc Duy Le,Jixue Liu,Yanchang Zhao,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级黑盒适配框架，通过两个即插即用包装器（SMR²和MBB）在推理时增强冻结的时间序列基础模型，无需访问或微调骨干参数。在澳大利亚南部多站点的一个月前瞻SPEI预测任务上，该方法实现了高达26%的MSE降低，适用于资源受限的区域气候预测场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型时间序列基础模型在零样本预测中表现优异，但应用于区域气候预测时面临三大实际约束：模型权重常为专有、本地训练记录有限、计算预算紧张，使得传统微调方法不可行。因此需要一种不访问骨干参数、不进行骨干微调的黑盒适配方法。

Method: 提出轻量级黑盒适配框架，包含两个即插即用包装器：(1) SMR²（平稳性感知多分辨率残差），将输入分解为多分辨率时间视图，学习跨步特定残差校正以捕获区域动态，自适应集成得到单一预测；(2) MBB（移动块自举），通过块重采样保留时间依赖性，对时间连贯的残差扰动进行集成以稳定点预测。两个包装器均遵循bagging原则：构建输入的多样视图，用同一冻结骨干预测，再聚合，所有适配来自推理时集成而非权重更新。

Result: 在澳大利亚南部多个站点的一个月前瞻标准化降水蒸散指数（SPEI）预测任务上，该框架在多个骨干模型上持续提升预测性能，相较于对应冻结骨干实现了高达26%的均方误差（MSE）降低。

Conclusion: 该框架在无需访问骨干参数和无需微调的条件下，通过推理时集成的轻量级方式有效增强了冻结时间序列基础模型的区域气候预测能力，为资源受限的区域预测系统提供了实用部署方案。

Abstract: Large \emph{Time Series Foundation Models} (TSFMs) demonstrate strong zero-shot forecasting capabilities across diverse domains. However, their application to regional climate forecasting faces practical challenges: model weights are often proprietary, local training records are limited, and computational budgets are constrained, making traditional fine-tuning approaches infeasible. To address these constraints, we introduce a lightweight, black-box adaptation framework (requiring no access to backbone parameters and no backbone fine-tuning) that enhances frozen TSFMs at inference time through two plug-and-play wrappers: \textbf{SMR\textsuperscript{2}} (Stationarity aware multi-resolution Residual), which decomposes the input into multi-resolution temporal views, learns stride specific residual corrections that capture regional dynamics, then adaptively ensembles them into a single forecast, and \textbf{MBB} (Moving Block Bootstrap), which preserves temporal dependencies through block resampling and ensembles over temporally coherent residual perturbations to stabilize the point forecast. Both wrappers instantiate the same bagging style principle: they build diverse views of the input or its residuals, forecast each with the same frozen backbone, and aggregate, so all adaptation comes from inference time ensembling rather than any weight update. Evaluated on one month ahead Standardized Precipitation Evapotranspiration Index (SPEI) prediction across multiple sites in South Australia, our framework consistently improves forecasting performance across several backbone models, demonstrating up to 26\% mean squared error (MSE) reduction over the corresponding frozen backbone while enabling practical deployment in resource constrained regional forecasting systems.

</details>


### [122] [After the Euclidean Highway: Hyperbolic Expert AI as the Next Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17513)
*Kwan Soo Shin,In Seok Kang,Munho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HySAT（双曲结构感知训练）方法，证明将双曲几何仅应用于损失层而非整个网络适配器可避免训练崩溃（17次崩溃，~220 GPU小时），通过六组专家SLM实验（Llama 3.1/EXAONE 3.5，18M样本，零NaN）和四项消融研究验证，并提供数学证明（三命题一引理）解释损失层稳定性。四模型已部署（一为生产级），附带完整故障日志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双曲方法错误地将曲率视为可调旋钮而非结构约束（"放置是法则，不是旋钮"），导致适配器层应用双曲几何时发生训练崩溃（17次）。需要找到曲率与梯度正确接触的位置。

Method: HySAT：仅在损失层应用双曲损失，其余网络保持欧几里得几何。在六组SLM（Llama 3.1/EXAONE 3.5）上进行四臂消融实验，配合18M样本语料库，通过三命题一引理数学证明损失层稳定性。

Result: 零NaN过~317K优化步；四模型操作部署（一为消费者级）；完整记录17次训练崩溃日志（Zenodo, CC-BY-4.0）；证明适配器上应用双曲几何必然不稳定，而损失层放置是唯一稳定位置。

Conclusion: 双曲几何的放置位置是关键设计决策——应仅置于损失层，避免破坏欧几里得网络的结构；同时指出"专家域是树结构，而欧几里得Transformer不是"这一根本性不匹配。

Abstract: Expert domains are trees; the Euclidean transformer is not, diluting parent-child structure exponentially at depth. The hyperbolic turn left one question unasked: not how much of a network to curve, but where curvature may touch the gradient. Placement is a law, not a knob: the same geometry on a trainable adapter collapses training (seventeen training collapses, ~220 GPU-hours), yet at the loss layer alone it trains without one -- this is HySAT (Hyperbolic Structure-Aware Training), hyperbolic losses at the loss layer only. Across six expert SLMs we constructed and deployed (Llama 3.1 and EXAONE 3.5; four adapter strategies; 18.0M-sample corpus; zero NaN over ~317K optimizer steps), a matched four-arm ablation isolates the preserved manifold invariant, and three propositions and a lemma prove why loss-only placement is stable where adapter-on-manifold is not. Four models are operationally deployed (one live, consumer-facing), two open-weight, with per-step traces and a seventeen-incident failure ledger on Zenodo (CC-BY-4.0).

</details>


### [123] [One-step lowest-variance selection in a Gaussian random-field model motivated by masked diffusion: Total correlation and a square root collision threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17522)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在随机高斯场模型中研究了掩蔽离散扩散中置信引导并行解掩码的单步选择机制，分析了基于得分最小的K个位置选择在依赖结构下的总相关性，建立了亚平方根和平方根两种尺度下的渐近理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解掩蔽离散扩散模型中置信引导并行解掩码的选择行为提供严格的随机几何理论基础，量化预算规模、得分依赖性和空间相关性如何共同影响基于置信度的选择。

Method: 构建一个具有局部依赖非负得分场的高斯随机场模型，采用距离依赖的高斯相关模型度量选择位置间的依赖关系，分别在亚平方根和平方根尺度下分析条件高斯总相关性的渐近行为。

Result: 在保守的亚平方根尺度下，选定块的条件高斯总相关性依概率趋于零；在平方根尺度下，总相关性以正渐近概率保持非可忽略，且存在严格正的期望下界。

Conclusion: 为理解掩蔽离散扩散中单步置信选择提供了理论基线，揭示了预算大小、得分依赖和空间相关性的联合作用机制。

Abstract: Motivated by confidence-guided parallel unmasking in masked discrete diffusion, we study a single selection step in a stylized Gaussian random-field model. A locally dependent nonnegative score field represents position wise uncertainty, and the scheduler selects the K positions with the smallest scores. Dependence among the selected positions is measured through a distance-dependent Gaussian correlation model. This separation provides a tractable framework for quantifying how the geometry of low-score locations affects the dependence cost of factorized parallel decoding. We establish two complementary results. In a conservative sub-square-root regime, the conditional Gaussian total correlation of the selected block vanishes in probability. At the square-root scale, it remains non-negligible with positive asymptotic probability and admits a strictly positive expectation lower bound. Synthetic experiments support the predicted finite-size behavior. These results provide a rigorous stochastic-geometry baseline for understanding how budget size, score dependence, and spatial correlation jointly shape one-step confidence-based selection in masked discrete diffusion.

</details>


### [124] [Program Synthesis for Simulation-Based Inference: Joint Model Selection and Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17540)
*Siddharth Mishra-Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个结合大语言模型进行程序合成与神经模拟推论的框架，实现从自然语言描述出发的联合模型选择与参数估计，能跨模型族进行模拟推演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经模拟推演需要用户指定固定模型结构的模拟器，无法自动探索和选择不同模型族，限制了其在模型发现与比较中的应用。

Method: 利用LLM从自然语言描述中生成候选模拟程序，通过反馈驱动变异进行迭代优化，并使用神经密度估计评估模型，实现跨模型的模拟推演。

Result: 在确定性动力学、随机流行病模型和暗物质子结构推断等基准任务上，该方法能够从开放提示中识别出合理的模型族，其准确度反映数据信息含量和候选模型的可辨识性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将LLM的程序合成能力与神经模拟推演结合，实现了从数据到模型族的自动推断，拓展了模拟推演方法在模型选择中的应用边界。

Abstract: Neural simulation-based inference enables parameter estimation for complex models, but typically requires the user to specify a simulator encoding a fixed model structure. We present a framework for joint model selection and parameter estimation that combines large language models for program synthesis with neural simulation-based inference. Given a natural language description of the system and data under investigation, an LLM proposes candidate simulator programs which are iteratively refined via feedback-driven mutation and evaluated using neural density estimation. The approach enables simulation-based inference over a pool of models, not just parameters within a fixed model. On benchmarks spanning deterministic dynamics, stochastic epidemic models, and dark matter substructure inference from gravitational-lensing images, the method identifies plausible model families from open-ended prompts, with accuracy that reflects the information content of the data and identifiability of candidate models.

</details>


### [125] [Volatility-Aware Extreme Event Detection in High-Frequency Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17555)
*Maorufa Zaman,Haris Md Sahed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种波动率感知的极端事件检测方法，通过将高波动率区间纳入目标定义，利用XGBoost模型在比特币高频限价订单簿数据上实现了PR-AUC从0.06到0.40的六倍提升，表明目标设计比模型复杂度更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频金融市场中极端价格变动难以预测，传统方法将极端事件视为孤立观测，忽视了波动率聚集等市场动态特性，导致对稀有但影响重大的事件检测效果不佳。

Method: 基于波动率聚类现象，将目标定义扩展为同时包含大未来收益和高波动率区间；使用XGBoost模型，结合时间序列交叉验证和不平衡感知评估。

Result: 所提方法PR-AUC约0.40，基线仅约0.06，实现了超过六倍的极端事件检测性能提升。

Conclusion: 目标设计在金融机器学习中具有关键作用，其影响往往超过模型复杂度；融入波动率结构的标注方法为高频加密货币市场极端事件检测提供了更有效且更现实的框架。

Abstract: Predicting extreme price movements in high-frequency financial markets is a challenging task due to non-stationarity, heavy-tailed return distributions, and severe class imbalance. In particular, rare but impactful events are often difficult to detect using conventional modeling approaches, which typically treat extreme movements as isolated observations. This study proposes a volatility-aware approach for extreme event detection using high-frequency Bitcoin limit order book (LOB) data. Motivated by empirical evidence of volatility clustering, the target formulation is extended to incorporate both large future returns and high-volatility regimes. This redefinition increases the proportion of informative samples and aligns the learning objective with the underlying market dynamics. Using a tree-based model (XGBoost) with time-series cross-validation and imbalance-aware evaluation, the proposed method achieves a Precision-Recall AUC of approximately 0.40, significantly outperforming the baseline formulation with a PR-AUC of around 0.06. This represents more than a sixfold improvement in detecting rare events. The results highlight that target design plays a critical role in financial machine learning, often exceeding the impact of model complexity. By incorporating volatility structure into the labeling process, the proposed approach provides a more effective and realistic framework for extreme event detection in high-frequency cryptocurrency markets.

</details>


### [126] [CoCurve: Cross-Module Co-Pruning Curvature for Training-Free Structured LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17568)
*Zhiren Gong,Zihao Zeng,Zijie Wang,Tiantong Wang,Chau Yuen,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoCurve是一种无需微调的校准方法，通过二阶泰勒展开和Fisher矩阵捕捉跨模块（注意力头和FFN通道组）的联合剪枝交互，将剪枝问题转化为单次二次规划求解，避免了独立评分忽略单元间依赖性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的结构化剪枝方法独立评分每个计算单元，隐含假设剪枝损失可加和，但这忽略了Transformer中通过共享残差流耦合的子层间依赖关系——两个单独弱单元可能共同不可或缺，独立评分会错误地将它们一起删除。

Method: 提出CoCurve方法：对冻结模型与其掩码副本之间的token级KL散度进行二阶泰勒展开，得到Fisher矩阵；其对角线为经典节点显著性，非对角线为共剪枝曲率边（联合移除两个单元的额外损失）；在单次消融可加性近似下，该矩阵退化为单单元消融特征的Gram乘积，仅需M次前向传播即可恢复完整M×M交互矩阵；最终剪枝简化为一个带共享预算的二次规划，单次求解。

Result: 无需标注数据、微调或恢复步骤，仅需校准即可完成剪枝，捕捉了跨模块的联合剪枝交互效应。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模注意力头和FFN通道组之间的联合剪枝依赖关系，CoCurve在无需微调的条件下实现了更准确的结构化剪枝。

Abstract: Structured pruning compresses large language models (LLMs) by removing whole computational units, such as attention heads and feed-forward (FFN) channel groups. Most training-free methods, however, rank these units independently, implicitly treating the loss from pruning a set as the sum of its individual losses. This view fails for Transformers, whose sublayers are coupled through a shared residual stream. Two individually weak units can thus be jointly indispensable, yet independent scoring is blind to such dependence and removes them together. We introduce CoCurve (Cross-Module Co-Pruning Curvature), a calibration-only, fine-tuning-free method that prunes attention and FFN units jointly. A second-order Taylor expansion of the token-level KL between the frozen model and its masked copy yields a single Fisher matrix whose diagonal is classical node saliency and whose off-diagonal entries are co-pruning curvature edges: the extra damage of removing two units together. Under a single-ablation additivity approximation this matrix reduces to a Gram product of single-unit ablation features, so the full M x M interaction is recovered from M forward passes, with no pairwise sweeps or gradients. Pruning then reduces to one budgeted quadratic program, solved in a single shot under a shared attention--FFN budget, with no labels, fine-tuning, or recovery.

</details>


### [127] [A Weisfeiler-Leman Characterization of Global-Attention Graph Transformers for Mixed-Integer Linear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17570)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Craig A. Knoblock,Jay Pujara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明具有全局注意力机制的图基础模型（GFMs）在表示混合整数线性规划（MILP）时，其表达能力被1-WL测试所约束：1-WL等价的MILP图会被映射到相同的嵌入表示。实验验证了十种不同编码器下这一结论的普遍性，并指出超越1-WL的表达能力来源于输入编码而非注意力机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究具有全局注意力的图基础模型在表示MILP问题时的表达能力边界，明确哪些MILP实例会被映射到相同的表示，从而理解这些模型的结构捕获能力。

Method: 通过图同构测试框架分析表达能力，采用组合性证明方法证明层次化图Transformer（结合全局线性注意力、边加权交叉注意力和二分图消息传递）的各组件均为对称多重集函数，因此保持1-WL等价性。并在10种编码器上进行实验验证。

Result: 所有测试编码器将1-WL等价的非同构图对映射到数值相同的嵌入；超越1-WL的表达能力来自随机游走位置编码等输入编码，而非注意力机制。

Conclusion: 全局注意力GFMs的表达能力受限于1-WL测试，这为理解其表示等价性提供了编码器无关的诊断工具。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) with global attention are increasingly used to represent mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs), aiming to capture structure beyond the locality of standard graph neural networks. We study their expressive power through graph isomorphism testing, asking which MILP instances they map to identical representations. We prove that a broad class of hierarchical graph transformers combining global linear attention, edge-weighted cross-attention, and bipartite message passing is bounded by the one-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman (1-WL) test: under any parameter setting, 1-WL-equivalent MILP graphs receive identical graph embeddings. Our compositional proof shows that each architectural component is a symmetric multiset function and thus preserves 1-WL equivalence. We validate this characterization across ten diverse graph encoders, including Graphormer-, GraphGPS-, Set-Transformer-, and Gasse-style models. Across model capacities, graph scales, and pooling operators, every tested encoder maps 1-WL-equivalent non-isomorphic graph pairs to numerically identical embeddings. Consequently, graph invariants that vary within a 1-WL equivalence class cannot be recovered from these representations. We further show that expressiveness beyond 1-WL arises from input encoding rather than attention: random-walk positional encodings separate the constructed pairs, while additional constructions expose the limits of this remedy. These results characterize the expressive power of global-attention GFMs and provide an encoder-agnostic diagnostic for detecting 1-WL-induced representation equivalence.

</details>


### [128] [AGG: Jacobian-Aggregated Group Gradient for Efficient GRPO Training of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17572)
*Ruiyi Ding,Jie Li,He Kang,Ziyan Liu,Chengru Song,Yuan chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAGG通过利用DiT隐状态沿采样轨迹的近似线性变化，将组策略优化的反向传播成本从每步W次全Transformer反向传播减少到仅2次，实现约2倍加速且质量损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO扩展到扩散模型时，每个采样时间步都需要通过高容量DiT骨干网络反向传播梯度，导致高分辨率文生图训练计算成本极高，亟需降低反向传播开销。

Method: 观察到DiT隐状态速度预测沿轨迹平滑且近似线性变化，提出Jacobian聚合组梯度（JAGG）：用端点Jacobian的t加权插值近似中间步Jacobian，将W步的上游信号聚合为两个复合梯度，通过一次联合反向传播完成。同时设计余弦相似度路由规则jagg_frac，仅在假设成立时启用JAGG。

Result: 在文生图基准上，JAGG实现约2倍反向传播加速，且生成质量几乎无下降。

Conclusion: JAGG有效利用DiT轨迹的线性特性大幅降低RL训练成本，为扩散模型的对齐训练提供了高效的梯度近似方案。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a powerful reinforcement learning algorithm for aligning generative models with human preferences. While successful in large language models~\cite{shao2024deepseekmathpushinglimitsmathematical}, its extension to diffusion and flow matching models introduces a severe computational bottleneck: gradients must be back-propagated through the high-capacity DiT backbone at \emph{every} timestep of the sampling trajectory, making high-resolution text-to-image (T2I) training prohibitively expensive. Training-free DiT inference acceleration methods (e.g., $Δ$-DiT, ScalingCache) exploit the fact that DiT hidden states and velocity predictions vary \emph{smoothly and nearly linearly} along the trajectory. We ask whether the same linearity can reduce the backward-pass cost of DiT RL training, and answer affirmatively with \textbf{JAGG} (\textbf{J}acobian-\textbf{A}ggregated \textbf{G}roup \textbf{G}radient), which reduces full transformer backward passes from $W$ to $2$ per group of $W$ consecutive steps. JAGG approximates intermediate-step Jacobians via $t$-weighted interpolation of the endpoint Jacobians, then aggregates per-step upstream signals into two composite gradients applied through a single joint backward pass. We prove this interpolation is \emph{exact} when the velocity is linear in $(z,t)$, and a cosine-similarity routing rule (\texttt{jagg\_frac}) deploys JAGG only where the assumption holds. Experiments on T2I benchmarks show JAGG delivers $\sim$2$\times$ backward speedup with negligible quality degradation.

</details>


### [129] [Concentration and Mean-Square Bounds for Contractive Stochastic Approximation: A Unified Elementary Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17595)
*Siddharth Chandak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一且初等的分析方法，用于建立随机逼近算法在任意范数压缩映射下的均方界和浓度界，解决了乘性噪声和潜在无界迭代带来的挑战，并首次给出了次高斯尾部的最大时间浓度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中算子往往在ℓ∞范数下压缩且噪声随迭代尺度缩放，但现有方法依赖光滑Lyapunov函数和多阶段自举论证，分析复杂。本文旨在提供一种更简洁、统一的证明框架。

Method: 利用平均噪声序列和对应的辅助迭代，直接建立误差范数的单步Lyapunov漂移不等式；均方界通过漂移不等式与归纳论证结合；浓度界通过概率归纳构造"好事件"序列，使标准Azuma-Hoeffding不等式适用，步长可依赖置信水平的对数。

Result: 首次得到了乘性噪声下SA算法的次高斯尾部最大时间浓度界；均方界表明迭代在期望意义下有界；方法具有一般性，可推广至其他噪声模型和迭代算法。

Conclusion: 本文的统一初等方法简化了分析，拓展了浓度界的理论边界，并为更广泛的迭代随机算法提供了可迁移的证明技术。

Abstract: We establish mean-square and concentration bounds for stochastic approximation (SA) with arbitrary norm contractive mappings, under a multiplicative noise model where the noise may scale affinely with the norm of the iterates, and the iterates are potentially unbounded. These settings arise in reinforcement learning, where operators are often contractive in the $\ell_\infty$ norm and the noise scales with the iterates. To address the arbitrary norm, earlier works replace the non-smooth squared norm with a smooth Lyapunov function constructed via the generalized Moreau envelope. For concentration analysis, these works handle multiplicative noise and unbounded iterates through a multi-stage bootstrapping argument that starts from a time-varying worst-case bound and iteratively refines it. We instead present a unified and elementary analysis that yields both bounds. Using an averaged noise sequence and corresponding auxiliary iterates, we obtain a one-step Lyapunov drift inequality for the normed error directly, without smoothing the norm or constructing an envelope. For the mean-square bound, we combine this drift inequality with an induction argument showing that the iterates remain bounded in expectation. For the concentration bound, we develop a probabilistic induction over a sequence of "good" events on which the iterates are controlled, allowing the standard Azuma-Hoeffding bound to be applied. Our approach yields the first sub-Gaussian tailed maximal (all-time) concentration bound for SA under multiplicative noise, by allowing the stepsize to depend logarithmically on the confidence level. Beyond the specific setting considered here, we discuss the generalizability of these proof techniques to other noise models and iterative algorithms.

</details>


### [130] [Trustworthy Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction via Reliability-Aware Multi-Engine Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17601)
*Yongchan Hong,Defu Cao,Wenjin Liu,Thomas Ku,Jordy Homing Lam,Emily Nguyen,Willie Neiswanger,Vsevolod Katritch,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RELIABLE-BA，一种基于证据融合的多引擎蛋白质-配体结合亲和度预测框架，通过Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布建模各引擎、从分子上下文学习可信度缩放认知不确定性、以及闭式聚合专家预测，在保持竞争性点预测的同时显著提升不确定性校准，并能在保留高置信度样本时将预测误差降低25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对接引擎在预测结合亲和度时经常产生不一致的结果，且缺乏可解释的置信度度量或不确定性分解，无法根据化学上下文区分预测的可信程度。共识评分和集成方法虽能提高平均准确率，但同等对待所有预测，忽略了每个蛋白质-配体对的化学特异性。

Method: (1) 将每个对接引擎建模为基于Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布的证据专家；(2) 从分子上下文中学习可信度来缩放认知不确定性，同时保留各专家的预测均值；(3) 通过闭式聚合融合专家预测，同时捕获个体不确定性和引擎间的不一致性。

Result: 在PDBBind和BDB2020+基准上达到竞争性点预测性能且不确定性校准显著提升；在SARS-CoV-2 Mpro和5HT2A受体数据集上验证了临床相关药物靶点的适用性；利用不确定性估计过滤高置信度样本可将预测误差降低25%。

Conclusion: RELIABLE-BA是首个将证据融合与上下文相关可信度结合的多引擎结合亲和度预测框架，为可信赖的AI驱动药物发现提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: Accurate protein-ligand binding affinity prediction is central to computational drug discovery, yet modern docking engines frequently disagree without indicating which prediction to trust. Consensus scoring and ensemble methods improve mean accuracy but treat all predictions identically without interpretable confidence measures or uncertainty decomposition, ignoring the chemical context of each protein-ligand pair. To address this limitation, we introduce RELIABLE-BA (RELIABiLity-aware Evidential fusion for Binding Affinity), an evidential framework for multi-engine binding affinity prediction. Our model comprises three steps: (1) modeling each engine as an evidential expert via Normal-Inverse-Gamma distributions, (2) scaling epistemic uncertainty through learned reliability from molecular context while preserving each expert's predictive mean, and (3) fusing experts through closed-form aggregation that captures both individual uncertainty and inter-engine disagreement. Experiments on the PDBBind and BDB2020+ benchmarks demonstrate competitive point prediction with substantially improved uncertainty calibration, and additional validation on the SARS-CoV-2 Mpro dataset and 5HT2A receptor demonstrates applicability to clinically relevant drug targets. Crucially, these uncertainty estimates enable reliable filtering of protein-ligand pairs, reducing prediction error by up to 25% when retaining only high-confidence pairs. To our knowledge, RELIABLE-BA is the first multi-engine binding affinity prediction framework to combine evidential fusion with context-dependent reliability, offering a principled path toward trustworthy AI-guided drug discovery. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yongchand/RELIABLE-BA.

</details>


### [131] [Optimizing the Preconditioner: A Black-box Online-to-Nonconvex Conversion with Static Regret Minimization Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17607)
*Haichen Hu,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将随机非凸优化黑盒归约为在线凸优化中静态遗憾的方法，通过维护可预测梯度跟踪器和在线学习器选择预条件器，实现了对光滑非凸目标和非光滑 Lipschitz 非凸目标的收敛保证，解决了 Chen 和 Hazan (2024) 提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非凸随机优化能否以黑盒方式归约为在线凸优化中普通静态遗憾的问题，回应 Chen 和 Hazan (2024) 提出的开放问题。

Method: 维护一个可预测梯度跟踪器，由黑盒在线学习器选择预条件器决定更新方向；在线学习器面对线性凸损失，基于单固定比较器的静态遗憾进行评估。该框架可扩展至 Lipschitz 非凸目标（无需梯度 Lipschitz 连续）。

Result: 对于 β-光滑目标，得到收敛率 O(σ√(Mβ)/√T + √(Mβ)R_T(A,I_d)/T + Mβ/T)；若 OCO 算法满足 R_T=O(√T)，则恢复经典 O(1/√T) 速率。对于 Lipschitz 非凸目标，当 OCO 预言机有平方根静态遗憾时，达到 Goldstein 稳定点的最优 O(T^{-2/7}) 收敛率。

Conclusion: 该框架将优化器设计分离为梯度预测和在线预条件器选择，提供了理解 AdaGrad、Shampoo 等自适应方法在非凸优化中应用的原理性视角。

Abstract: We study whether stochastic nonconvex optimization can be reduced to ordinary static regret minimization in online convex optimization in a black-box manner. For smooth nonconvex objectives, our reduction maintains a predictable gradient tracker, while a black-box online learner selects a preconditioner that determines how this tracker is transformed into the update direction. The learner receives linear convex losses and is evaluated against a single fixed comparator over one undiscounted online game. For a $β$-smooth objective with range bounded by $M$ and an unbiased stochastic-gradient oracle with variance bounded by \(σ^2\), we establish $$\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^T
  \mathbb E\!\left[\|\nabla f(x_t)\|_2^2\right]
  \lesssim
  \frac{σ\sqrt{Mβ}}{\sqrt T}
  +
  \frac{\sqrt{Mβ}\,
  \mathscr R_T(\mathcal A,I_d)}{T}
  +
  \frac{Mβ}{T}.$$ Consequently, any black-box OCO algorithm with $\mathscr R_T(\mathcal A,I_d)=O(\sqrt T)$ recovers the classical $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ convergence rate.
  We further show that the same black-box framework extends beyond the smooth setting to Lipschitz nonconvex objectives without Lipschitz continuous gradients. Importantly, this extension continues to rely only on an ordinary static-regret guarantee and requires no stronger notion of online regret. When the OCO oracle admits square-root static regret, the resulting conversion achieves the optimal $O(T^{-2/7})$ convergence rate for the corresponding Goldstein stationary point. These results resolve the open problem posed by Chen and Hazan (2024). More broadly, our framework separates optimizer design into gradient prediction and online preconditioner selection, providing a principled perspective on how adaptive optimization methods such as AdaGrad and Shampoo may be understood through static regret and applied in nonconvex optimization.

</details>


### [132] [PoLoRA: A Preconditioned Orthogonalized LoRA Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17620)
*Nikhil Ghosh,Tetiana Parshakova,Robert M. Gower*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PoLoRA，一种针对LoRA微调的结构感知优化器，通过乘积感知的谱更新方向、曲率预条件和幅度控制规则，在1B-8B参数模型上以1.2-1.7倍更少步骤达到Adam的最终损失，且每步开销不超过3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA优化方法（Adam忽略矩阵和乘积结构，Muon及其变体不能一致优于Adam）未能充分利用LoRA的参数结构，需要一种能感知LoRA矩阵乘积结构的优化器以实现更高效的微调。

Method: PoLoRA包含三个核心组件：(1) 乘积感知的谱更新方向，利用矩阵乘积结构；(2) 曲率预条件，通过控制每个样本损失变化推导；(3) 幅度规则，同时控制因子矩阵和合并后更新的幅度。

Result: 在代码和数学指令微调任务上（1B-8B参数模型），PoLoRA达到调优Adam最终损失所需步数减少1.2-1.7倍，每步额外开销不超过3%；对学习率更不敏感，且最优学习率在不同秩下保持稳定。

Conclusion: PoLoRA通过显式利用LoRA的乘积结构，在收敛速度和鲁棒性上优于忽略结构的Adam和Muon变体，为LoRA微调提供了更高效的优化方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) makes finetuning large language models cheaper by adding to each weight matrix a trainable low-rank update parameterized as the product of two matrices. These matrices are usually trained with Adam, which treats them as a single flat vector of parameters and ignores both the matrix and product structure of LoRA. Applying a matrix-aware optimizer such as Muon to each factor does not consistently improve over Adam, and neither do the product-aware Muon variants proposed in concurrent works. To realize consistent gains, we introduce PoLoRA, a Preconditioned Orthogonalized LoRA optimizer built from three ingredients: a product-aware spectral update direction, curvature preconditioning derived from controlling the per-sample loss change, and a magnitude rule that controls the sizes of both the factor and merged updates. We evaluate PoLoRA on instruction-tuning datasets for code and math across models from 1B to 8B parameters, and find that it reaches the final held-out loss achieved by tuned Adam in 1.2-1.7 times fewer steps, while adding at most 3% per-step overhead. Compared to Adam, PoLoRA is also less sensitive to the learning rate, and its optimal learning rate is stable across ranks.

</details>


### [133] [Can Transformers Really Do It All? On the Compatibility of Inductive Biases Across Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17624)
*Damien Teney,Liangze Jiang,Hemanth Saratchandran,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种优化Transformer架构的方法，通过用学习到的函数替换关键非线性层（GeLU、softmax），发现标准Transformer很少是架构空间中的局部最优，任务特定架构可大幅提升性能但牺牲通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标准Transformer对于特定任务或数据集是否最优，以及是否存在更好的任务特定归纳偏置，推动AI超越仅仅缩放现有设计的范式。

Method: 提出一种优化Transformer架构的方法：将最重要的非线性函数（GeLU、softmax）替换为在保留数据上学到的函数；然后在其他数据集上训练得到的架构，以评估任务间的兼容性。

Result: 在算法玩具任务上，新架构在学习速度、分布内外泛化及种子稳定性方面有显著提升，但高度任务特定；在代码和语言建模数据集上，改进一致但幅度较小，且跨数据集/领域迁移性更好。

Conclusion: 标准Transformer很少是架构空间中的局部最优，存在更优但牺牲通用性的简单替代方案，暗示有空间设计同时支持多种能力（如流畅性和鲁棒推理）的改进架构。

Abstract: Transformers are remarkably versatile and their design is largely consistent across a variety of applications. But are they optimal for any given task or dataset? The answer may be key for pushing AI beyond merely scaling current designs.
  *Method.* We present a method to optimize a transformer architecture for a given dataset, which we use as a tool to study optimal task-specific inductive biases. This method replaces the most important non-linearities (GeLUs,;softmax) with functions learned on held-out data. We then train the resulting architectures on other datasets, as a way to evaluate the compatibility between pairs of tasks.
  Findings. On algorithmic toy tasks, we identify new architectures with dramatic improvements in learning speed, in- and out-of-distribution generalization, and stability across seeds. The new designs prove very task-specific however, and indicate that these tasks require inductive biases very different from those of standard transformers. On code and language modeling datasets, we also find architectures with consistent, yet smaller improvements. These designs transfer much better across datasets and domains (English & computer code).
  Implications. Our results show that standard transformers are rarely a local optimum in the space of architectures. Simple alternatives can perform much better but sacrifice universality. This suggests that there may be room for improved architectures that better support multiple capabilities simultaneously, such as fluency and robust reasoning.

</details>


### [134] [TypiCore: A Hybrid Active Query Strategy for Class-Incremental Learning on Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17632)
*Gabor Szucs,Samuel Jacsev,Marcell Nemeth,Davide Dalle Pezze,Gian Antonio Susto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多变量时间序列的主动类增量学习（ACIL），并提出TypiCore混合查询策略，该策略在主动学习周期中交替使用典型性和多样性样本选择，在有限标注预算下构建既具代表性又具多样性的记忆缓冲区，显著优于现有方法，甚至在某些数据集上匹配或超越全监督持续学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列数据流中标签稀缺，现有持续学习方法依赖全标注假设，导致实际应用受限。亟需探索在有限标注预算下如何进行有效的类增量学习。

Method: 提出TypiCore混合查询策略，在主动学习周期中交替进行典型性（typicality-based）和多样性（diversity-based）样本选择，构建代表性且多样化的记忆缓冲区。系统评估多种查询策略与回放方法组合在多变量时间序列基准上的表现。

Result: TypiCore在TSCIL基准上取得统计学显著改进，优于所有基线方法，在多个数据集上匹配或超越全监督持续学习性能，同时仅需消耗部分标注预算。

Conclusion: 不确定性方法和分布感知方法在受限标注预算下性能有限，而交替利用典型性与多样性的混合策略能有效提升标签效率与模型性能，为时间序列主动类增量学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Time series data play a pivotal role across numerous domains, including healthcare and manufacturing. In real-world environments, models must cope with distribution shifts over time, a challenge commonly addressed through Continual Learning (CL) techniques. However, existing CL methods face a critical limitation: real-world data streams are rarely fully labeled, making annotation cost a major practical constraint. This paper investigates Active Class-Incremental Learning (ACIL) for multivariate time series, where a model must sequentially learn new classes while selectively querying labels under a fixed annotation budget. We present a systematic evaluation of a wide range of query strategies combined with multiple rehearsal-based approaches, assessing their impact on plasticity, stability, and label efficiency across four benchmark datasets. Our analysis reveals the limitations of uncertainty-based and distribution-aware methods in achieving strong performance under constrained labeling budgets. To address these shortcomings, we propose TypiCore, a novel hybrid query strategy that alternates between typicality-based and diversity-based sample selection across active learning cycles, enabling the construction of memory buffers that are both representative and diverse. Evaluated on the TSCIL benchmark, TypiCore delivers statistically significant improvements over all baselines and matches or surpasses fully supervised continual learning performance on multiple datasets while requiring a fraction of the available labels.

</details>


### [135] [Selectivity Matters: Source Node Influence Pruning for Unsupervised Graph Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17668)
*Ridong Han,Yawen Shen,Zhongnian Li,Tongfeng Sun,Xinzheng Xu,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有无监督图域自适应方法默认所有源节点都对对齐有正面贡献，但结构异常节点会导致负迁移。本文提出SNIP框架，通过多中心性度量量化源节点结构差异，筛选出结构兼容的节点构建子源图，从而将研究范式从特征对齐转向数据级精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UGDA方法假设所有源节点都对特征对齐有正面贡献，但忽略了节点语义与拓扑结构耦合的特性。结构偏移导致某些源节点（如结构异常点）在目标图中缺乏语义对应，强制对齐会引入噪声并造成负迁移。

Method: 提出SNIP框架：1) 集成多中心性度量量化源节点与目标域的结构差异；2) 基于排序的归一化机制消除不同度量间的尺度差异；3) 识别并过滤低影响力（结构不兼容）的源节点，构建精炼的"子源图"用于后续对齐。该方法为模型无关、数据为中心的即插即用框架。

Result: 在5个真实数据集的8个迁移场景中，SNIP一致优于竞争基线，显著提升自适应性能，验证了选择性节点利用优于全图训练。

Conclusion: 选择性源节点利用优于全图训练，将研究范式从特征级对齐转向数据级精炼是解决图域自适应中负迁移问题的有效方向。

Abstract: Unsupervised Graph Domain Adaptation (UGDA) aims to facilitate knowledge transfer from a labeled source graph to an unlabeled target graph by mitigating cross-domain distribution shifts. Existing methods primarily focus on node-level feature alignment in latent spaces, relying on the implicit assumption that all source nodes contribute positively to the alignment. However, this assumption often fails because a node's semantic information is intrinsically coupled with its topological graph structure. Due to structural shifts, source nodes with severe structural deviations (e.g., structural outliers) lack semantic counterparts in the target graph, and forcing alignment on them introduces severe noise and causes negative transfer. To bridge this gap, we argue that selective source node utilization is superior to full-graph training, thereby shifting the research paradigm from feature-level alignment to data-level refinement. To this end, we propose Source Node Influence Pruning (SNIP), a novel model-agnostic, data-centric refinement framework. Specifically, SNIP quantifies the structural discrepancy between individual source nodes and the target domain by integrating multiple centrality measures, assigning each source node an influence score. A rank-based normalization mechanism is further employed to eliminate scale variations across different measures, allowing SNIP to effectively identify and filter out structurally incompatible nodes with low influence scores. As a plug-and-play method, SNIP constructs a refined "sub-source" graph that is inherently more beneficial for subsequent alignment. Comprehensive experiments across eight transfer scenarios on five real-world datasets demonstrate that SNIP consistently outperforms competitive baselines and significantly enhances adaptation performance, validating the superiority of selective node utilization over full-graph training.

</details>


### [136] [GeneSpeak-FP: Target and Compound Retrieval from Observed Cell-Level Perturbation Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17671)
*Kseniia Vaniushkina,Jeongmin Lim,Jinyong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的检索模型，用于从单细胞扰动图谱中预测已知靶点和化合物。该模型将细胞扰动特征编码为目标检索向量和分子嵌入向量，在Tahoe-100M数据集上验证，目标Recall@10为0.408，化合物Hit@1为0.129，但泛化到未见化合物和细胞环境的能力尚未验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用大规模单细胞扰动图谱，解决逆问题：给定观察到的转录响应，从固定库中检索最一致的已注释靶点和化合物。

Method: 提出Transformer检索模型，输入为细胞级扰动特征（处理细胞与DMSO对照对比），编码器映射为目标检索向量和分子嵌入向量，联合训练有监督的靶点损失和结构-转录组对齐。采用带内化合物分层90/10条件对划分的评估策略。

Result: 在38,400个验证细胞上，目标Recall@10为0.408，Recall@20为0.544；化合物Hit@1为0.129，Hit@10为0.343，平均倒数排名为0.205。诊断评估显示主要模型与随机向量对照和基因袋对照相比有显著提升。

Conclusion: 单一多任务模型能从观察到的细胞级响应中恢复已注释的靶点和化合物身份。但在未见化合物和细胞环境下的泛化能力仍有待验证。

Abstract: Large-scale single-cell perturbation atlases make it possible to ask an inverse question: given an observed transcriptional response, which annotated targets and compounds in a fixed library are most consistent with that response? We present \model, a Transformer retrieval model for this closed-library setting. Each input is a cell-level perturbation signature formed by contrasting one treated cell with a cell-line-specific mean DMSO reference. The encoder maps the signature to a target-retrieval vector and a molecular-embedding vector, trained jointly with supervised target losses and structure--transcriptome alignment. We evaluate on Tahoe-100M conditions with mapped target annotations using a within-compound stratified 90/10 condition-pair split of 10,505 training and 1,168 validation drug--cell-line pairs. Because compounds and cell lines can occur in both partitions, the experiment measures held-out condition-pair retrieval rather than generalization to unseen compounds or cellular contexts. In a Monte Carlo evaluation over 38,400 sampled validation cells, \model\ achieved target Recall@10 of 0.408 and Recall@20 of 0.544, together with compound Hit@1 of 0.129, Hit@10 of 0.343, and mean reciprocal rank of 0.205 over a 379-compound bank. A separate diagnostic evaluation produced nearly identical values for the main model and large gains over a random-vector control and post-hoc bag-of-genes controls. These results demonstrate that a single multi-task model can recover both mapped target annotations and recorded compound identities from observed cell-level responses in the evaluated Tahoe-100M closed-library setting. Generalization to unseen compounds and cellular contexts remains to be established.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Objective Expressivity: Geometry Preservation in Multimodal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17673)
*Tillmann Rheude,Roland Eils,Benjamin Wild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在多模态对比学习中，编码器雅可比矩阵的条件数是影响高维对齐性能的关键因素。通过引入几何保持编码器（GPE）——利用正则化改善雅可比条件数，并采用LeakyReLU激活和残差路径等简单修改——在多个数据集和对比目标上显著提升了检索和线性探测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习正从图像-文本对扩展到三模态或更高阶的多模态对齐场景，但现有模型在从成对扩展到高阶对齐时面临优化和表示上的挑战，需要理解并解决这些挑战背后的关键因素。

Method: 通过理论分析识别出编码器雅可比条件数是关键因素，提出几何保持编码器（GPE），通过雅可比正则化直接改善条件数，并发现LeakyReLU激活函数和残差路径等简单修改即可恢复几何优势。

Result: 在合成基准和四个真实世界数据集（含缺失模态场景）上，改善雅可比条件数在多种对比目标下均提升了检索和线性探测性能；而表达能力强的目标在线性探测中收益有限。

Conclusion: 多模态对比学习的性能不仅依赖于目标函数的表达能力，还取决于底层编码器的几何性质和优化特性。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is increasingly moving toward settings with three or more modalities instead of image-text pairs. Yet, extending models from pairwise to higher-order multimodal alignment can introduce optimization and representation challenges. We identify encoder Jacobian conditioning as a key factor in trimodal contrastive learning: poorly conditioned encoders exhibit collapsing or amplified singular-value spectra, leading to exploding Jacobian condition numbers and degraded multimodal alignment. We introduce geometry-preserving encoders (GPEs) by directly conditioning the Jacobian through regularization and demonstrating that simple modifications like LeakyReLU activations and residual paths recover these geometric benefits. Across a synthetic benchmark and four real-world datasets including missing modalities, improving Jacobian conditioning boosts retrieval and linear probe performance across multiple contrastive objectives, whereas expressive objectives yield little benefit in linear probes. More broadly, our results show that multimodal contrastive learning depends not only on objective expressivity, but also on the geometric and optimization properties of the underlying encoders.

</details>


### [138] [Uncovering Latent Reasoning Strategies in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17674)
*Awni Altabaa,John Lafferty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将预训练语言模型的响应分布分解为结构化、策略条件表示的方法，通过路由器-生成器架构和基于信息增益的变分目标，解决后验坍塌问题，在多项策略任务上恢复与参考策略对齐的隐编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在推理任务中隐含多种策略，但这些策略在响应分布中纠缠不清，缺乏结构化表示。现有方法无法有效分解这些隐式策略，且标准变分推断会导致后验坍塌。

Method: 提出潜变量分解框架 p_θ(y|x) → (r_φ(z|x), g_φ(y|x,z))，其中路由器r将输入映射到策略分布z，生成器g条件于z生成响应。为克服后验坍塌，设计基于分数信息增益的变分目标，聚焦高基模型困惑度token的重建压力，促使z编码策略相关的响应变化。

Result: 在构建的多策略算法任务基准上，该方法生成的隐编码与参考策略高度对齐，同时保持了基模型的响应分布不变。

Conclusion: 所提目标有效恢复了与策略对齐的隐编码，为分解语言模型隐式推理策略提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A language model $p_θ(y \mid x)$ trained on reasoning tasks learns to solve problems via multiple distinct strategies, yet these strategies are implicit and entangled within the model's response distribution. We study the problem of decomposing the response distribution of a given pretrained language model into a structured, strategy-conditioned representation. Specifically, we learn a latent-variable factorization $p_θ(y \mid x) \leadsto (r_φ(z \mid x), g_φ(y \mid x,z))$, where a router $r$ maps each input to a distribution over latent strategies $z$ and a generator $g$ produces the response conditioned on that strategy. A key challenge is that the generator, initialized from the base model, already represents $p_θ(y \mid x)$ without using $z$. Standard variational inference therefore gives the model no incentive to route information through $z$ and can yield a severe form of posterior collapse. To address this, we propose a variational objective that measures fractional information gain relative to the base model's response loss and concentrates reconstruction pressure on tokens with high base model surprisal, encouraging $z$ to encode strategy-relevant response variation. We introduce a benchmark of multi-strategy algorithmic tasks and show that this objective recovers latent codes aligned with distinct reference strategies while preserving the base model's response distribution.

</details>


### [139] [Planning with Transformers: Chain of Computation and Structured Context Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17710)
*Ehsan Futuhi,Nathan R. Sturtevant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain of Computation (COC)架构，将基于Transformer的语言模型置于迭代循环中，利用结构化上下文窗口(SCW)解决规划问题。实验表明，即使是从头训练的小型LM也能在BlocksWorld和Pancake puzzle上达到超过99.89%的成功率，并能在最多20个圆盘的汉诺塔问题上解决需要超过100万步动作的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在许多领域表现优异，但在规划问题上表现不佳，而理论上Transformer具有图灵完备的计算能力。本文旨在探究这种理论与实际规划性能之间的差距，并提出一种能充分发挥LM模式匹配能力的计算架构。

Method: 提出Chain of Computation (COC)架构，包含：(1) 将基于Transformer的LM置于迭代循环中；(2) 使用结构化上下文窗口(SCW)提供恒定大小的上下文窗口，支持每步规划时选择使用哪个窗口；(3) LM在该架构中学习规划策略、预测世界模型并执行规划所需的算术运算。针对汉诺塔问题，进一步采用符号算术支持或确定性下推自动机(PDA)形式的SCW来减少训练数据需求。

Result: (1) 使用仅追加的SCW（类似图灵机磁带），从头训练的小型LM即可学习规划策略，并在BlocksWorld和Pancake puzzle上达到超过99.89%的成功率；(2) COC能解决最多20个圆盘的汉诺塔问题，需要超过100万个动作；(3) 失败案例分析表明汉诺塔问题的失败源于算术运算或遇到未见过的token；(4) 通过符号算术支持或PDA形式的SCW可大幅减少所需训练数据。

Conclusion: 通过COC架构，可以弥合LLMs理论计算能力与实际规划性能之间的差距。将LM置于适当计算结构中，即使规模较小的模型也能解决复杂规划问题，且能泛化到训练实例范围之外的长序列规划任务。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have had a remarkable impact across many areas of machine learning. However, recent studies have shown that they struggle to reliably solve planning problems. At the same time, theoretical results have shown that transformers, the core architecture underlying modern LLMs, are Turing-complete. In this work, we investigate this apparent gap between the theoretical computational power of LLMs and their empirical planning performance. We propose Chain of Computation (COC), a computational architecture that places a transformer-based LM inside an iterative loop, leveraging its strength as a pattern-matching system. The COC uses a Structured Context Window (SCW) which provides a constant-sized context window with support for choosing which window is used at each planning step. Within this architecture, the LM is able to learn a planning policy, predicts the world model, and performs the arithmetic operations required during planning. We show that, when given an append-only SCW (resembling a Turing Machine tape), even relatively small LMs trained from scratch can learn planning policies and generalize from a small number of training instances within each planning domain, achieving success rates above 99.89\% on BlocksWorld and the Pancake puzzle. Our analysis of failure cases in Tower of Hanoi (TOH) reveals that they arise from arithmetic operations or from encountering previously unseen tokens. We show that COC can solve TOH problem instances with up to 20 disks, requiring over 1 million actions, while requiring substantially less training data by either (1) planning with symbolical support for arithmetic or by (2) using a deterministic pushdown automaton (PDA) formulation for the SCW.

</details>


### [140] [MXSens: Sensitivity-Aware Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17733)
*Simla Burcu Harma,Danila Mishin,Zhengyuan Su,Ayan Chakraborty,Elizaveta Kostenok,Dongho Ha,Babak Falsafi,Martin Jaggi,Yunho Oh,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MXSens提出了一种基于敏感度引导的混合尾数位宽分配方法，利用MXINT的块结构在W4A4KV4设置下显著提升了LLM量化精度，在LLaMA-2-70B和LLaMA-3-8B上分别达到3.77和7.63的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4比特量化虽高效但受异常值影响精度下降严重；基于数据旋转或混合精度整数量化的方法依赖软件管理的缩放和频繁反量化，开销大；微缩放格式（如MXINT）在硬件中编码缩放因子，但与旋转方法不兼容。需要一种兼顾精度与效率的新方法。

Method: MXSens是一种无需训练的方法，基于列级和层级的敏感度分析（异常值严重程度和量化敏感性不均分布），为不同列和层分配混合尾数位宽（4/6/8），天然适配MXINT的块结构。

Result: 在W4A4KV4设置下，LLaMA-2-70B困惑度3.77，LLaMA-3-8B困惑度7.63（WikiText-2），优于现有基线方法。在多种模型和任务上均超越SOTA。

Conclusion: MXSens建立了LLM量化中精度与资源效率的新平衡，为硬件友好的混合精度量化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization enables efficient LLM inference, but suffers from significant accuracy degradation due to outliers. Prior work addresses this problem via data rotation or mixed-precision integer quantization, but often relies on software-managed scaling and frequent dequantization, incurring substantial overhead. Microscaling formats, such as MXINT, eliminate these inefficiencies by encoding scales in hardware, yet remain incompatible with rotation-based methods. Our analysis reveals that outliers vary in severity, from rare extremes to frequent mild deviations, and that quantization sensitivity is unevenly distributed across layers and columns. These insights motivate a fine-grained, sensitivity-guided approach. We introduce MXSens, a training-free method that assigns mixed mantissa bitwidths (4/6/8) based on column- and layer-wise sensitivity, naturally leveraging the block-wise structure of MXINT. MXSens outperforms state-of-the-art quantization methods across a range of models and tasks. Under the W4A4KV4 setting, MXSens achieves perplexities of 3.77 and 7.63 on LLaMA-2-70B and LLaMA-3-8B, respectively, substantially improving over existing baselines on WikiText-2. Our work establishes a new balance between accuracy and resource efficiency for LLM quantization.

</details>


### [141] [Towards Reliable Zero-Shot Crowd Forecasting: Evaluating Time Series Foundation Models for Special Event Pedestrian Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17758)
*Ziteng Li,Yanan Xin,Tina Comes,Serge Hoogendoorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索使用预训练时间序列基础模型，对大型特殊事件中的人群流动进行零样本概率预测，无需大量本地重训练即可提供可靠的实时预测和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型特殊事件中人群流动预测面临历史数据稀缺、数据分布异质、观测窗口短等挑战，传统监督方法效果有限，且需要同时提供准确的点估计和有效的不确定性量化，以支持运营决策和公共安全管理。

Method: 采用预训练时间序列基础模型作为轻量级方法，在无需大量本地重训练的情况下进行零样本概率预测。以SAIL2025事件为案例，使用面向短期事件的决策导向指标，对两种时间序列基础模型进行综合评估。

Result: 两种时间序列基础模型在SAIL2025事件的人群流动零样本概率预测上表现出一定效果，能够捕捉突发波动性和尾部风险，为人群管理者提供了操作上可靠的预测。
